below) because the server partial-merges a\n             patch onto the CURRENT saved settings, and setting mode="color"\n             alone before any color is ever chosen (or vice versa) fails\n             effective_container_settings\'s own validation and silently\n             reverts to "transparent" — sending both every time keeps them\n             consistent regardless of which control the merchant touched,\n             matching the legacy form\'s own whole-form-submit behavior. Only\n             ONE pattern exists in the legacy form too (a hidden\n             "commerce-doodle" value, never a merchant choice) — reused\n             verbatim, never a new pattern registry UI. Only\n             ONE pattern exists in the legacy form too (a hidden\n             "commerce-doodle" value, never a merchant choice) — reused\n             verbatim, never a new pattern registry UI. #}\n          <label class="r4-field-sublabel">پس\u200cزمینه چیدمان</label>\n          <div data-r4-container-background>\n            <select class="r4-control"
